* fix: address PR #178 third-round review (Pygments break, CodeBlock static, head inject, spec layer note)

Resolves the two spec/code-quality items flagged by the third-round
OpenCode review of PR #178.

Spec violation (🔴 Must Fix):

- Pygments adapter break-in-loop bug: in
  webcompy/ui/code_block/lexers/_adapters/_pygments.py, the inner
  _MAP iteration has its 'break' at the wrong indent level so it
  always fires after the first iteration. The result is that the
  mapping loop only ever considers the first _MAP entry, and any
  'if pygtok in src:' miss silently drops the token. Indenting the
  'break' under the 'if' block so the loop only terminates on a
  match. Add a regression test that uses a fake Pygments lexer to
  verify Keyword / String / Name.Function each map correctly.

Code quality (🟡 Should Improve):

- CodeBlock static path: add an early-return for static (non-Signal)
  'code' props. The current implementation always wraps the code in
  a Signal and creates a Computed, which is wasteful when the value
  never changes. The static path now mirrors the SyntaxHighlighting
  docs_app wrapper: call highlight() once at setup time and emit a
  raw string into the <code> child. No new runtime primitives; the
  public API is unchanged. Add 4 new tests in
  tests/test_code_block_component.py covering the static branch's
  VDOM shape, the explicit-lang case, the dynamic-branch SignalBase
  return value, and a byte-for-byte match with highlight().

- HeadElement.append_style closure capture: the _subscribe_callback
  previously called inject(DOM_PORT_KEY) on every signal update. If
  the render context is disposed before a late signal fires, the
  callback raises InjectionError. Resolve the DOM port once at
  registration time and capture it in the closure's default-arg
  list (matching the set_html_attr pattern). Add a regression test
  that registers a callback, disposes the DI scope, and then
  triggers the signal — the callback MUST NOT raise.

- css-architecture spec layer-order note: clarify the existing
  '@layer reset, tokens, components, webcompy-scope' Requirement to
  state explicitly that the layer list is intentionally minimal,
  that <style data-webcompy-dynamic> and <style data-webcompy-cid-rx>
  are emitted unlayered by design, and add a Scenario
  ('Dynamic and reactive styles are not in the layer list') to make
  the contract testable.

* fix: address PR #178 fourth-round review (theme spec, layer spec, cookie port, dual-creation)

Resolves the two spec-compliance items and the two code-quality items
flagged by the fourth-round OpenCode review of PR #178.

Spec violations (🔴 Must Fix):

- theme-system spec describes unimplemented data-theme design: the
  canonical openspec/specs/theme-system/spec.md was written for a
  design that mutates a data-theme HTML attribute, but the
  implementation injects a reactive <style data-webcompy-dynamic>
  element via app.append_style() instead. Rewrite the ThemeManager,
  server-cookie, client-cookie, and 'single source of truth'
  Requirements and Scenarios to describe the actual reactive-CSS
  approach. Drop every reference to data-theme on <html>.

- css-architecture spec has internal contradiction: the layer-order
  Requirement claimed that BOTH app.append_style outputs AND
  reactive_scoped_style outputs are emitted unlayered, but a
  separate Requirement mandates that all scoped_style rules are
  wrapped in @layer webcompy-scope. Reword the layer-order
  Requirement so that only app.append_style output
  (<style data-webcompy-dynamic>) is unlayered, and scoped_style /
  reactive_scoped_style output is explicitly layered
  (@layer webcompy-scope). Rename the second Scenario to
  'Dynamic styles are not in the layer list' so the testable
  contract is unambiguous.

Code quality (🟡 Should Improve):

- ServerCookiePort was never populated with request cookies: the
  RenderContext created ServerCookiePort() with no cookie header,
  so any server-side code calling inject(COOKIE_PORT_KEY).get(...)
  would see an empty dict even when the request had a webcompy-theme
  cookie. Thread a new cookie_header: str | None = None kwarg
  through app.create_render_context() and RenderContext.__init__(),
  and pass it to ServerCookiePort(cookie_header=...). The CLI
  server route handlers now read request.headers.get('cookie', '')
  once and forward it to both the existing initial_theme path and
  the new cookie_header path. Add 3 tests in
  tests/test_render_context_cookie_header.py covering populated
  header, missing header, and empty string.

- append_style callback dual-created the dynamic style element:
  the _subscribe_callback previously called _dom.create_element()
  + head_el.appendChild() when the element did not yet exist,
  duplicating the element-creation path in _render(). Make the
  callback a pure update path: if the element is missing, the
  callback silently no-ops and the next _render() pass will
  create it with the current value. _render() is now the single
  source of truth for <style data-webcompy-dynamic> creation.

Verified: 1237 unit tests pass (+3 from previous), 0 pyright
errors, ruff clean, webcompy generate still produces a working
static site.

* fix: address PR #178 fifth-round review (multi-instance, remove API, unknown lang, spec refs)

Resolves the two spec/code-quality items flagged by the fifth-round
OpenCode review of PR #178 (the review verdict was \u2705 Approved; these
are follow-ups before the next release).

Spec/code (🔴 Must Fix):

- Multi-instance reactive scoped style subscription bug: when the
  same ReactiveScopedStyle instance is used by multiple component
  instances of the same component, the second instance's
  use_reactive_scoped_style call short-circuited at
  'if style._cid == self._generator._id: return' and never
  registered its on_before_destroy hook. If the first instance was
  destroyed, the subscription was disposed, leaving the second
  instance with no live DOM updates. Refactor ReactiveScopedStyle
  to track a ref count; create the subscription on the first use
  per (style, generator) pair; register a per-instance dispose
  hook that decrements the ref count and disposes the
  subscription only when the count reaches zero. Add a regression
  test covering the multi-instance lifecycle.

Spec consistency (🔴 Must Fix):

- app-styles spec lacked cross-reference to css-architecture: the
  'Dynamic styles are emitted unlayered' Requirement referenced
  <style data-webcompy-dynamic> but did not link to
  openspec/specs/css-architecture/spec.md where the layer-order
  contract is defined. Add a Note at the top of the spec pointing
  to the css-architecture spec as the source of truth for the
  @layer cascade order and the rule that dynamic styles are
  intentionally unlayered.

Code quality (🟡 Should Improve):

- No API to remove a reactive scoped style: add
  context.remove_reactive_scoped_style(style) that drops the
  style from the generator's _reactive_styles list, decrements
  the ref count, and disposes the subscription if no other
  instance is using the style. The style is also marked with
  _removed=True so the head element rendering can skip it. Add
  the API to the ComponentContext Protocol. The head element
  rendering (both browser and SSR paths) now checks
  style.is_removed and skips removed styles. Known limitation:
  already-emitted <style data-webcompy-cid-rx='...'> elements
  are left in place until the next full head render pass.

- docs_app/static/styles/tokens.css duplicates framework tokens:
  the :root block duplicates webcompy/ui/_styles/tokens.css and
  the :root[data-theme='dark'] block is dead code because the
  framework uses a reactive <style data-webcompy-dynamic> rather
  than a data-theme attribute. Add a CSS comment at the top of
  the file explaining the duplication (intentional fallback for
  standalone builds) and that the dark block is a no-op when the
  framework is active.

- CodeBlock crashed on unknown language: highlight() propagated
  LexerNotFoundError to the caller, crashing the page on the
  first unknown 'lang' value. Catch the lookup error in
  highlight() and fall back to a single
  <span class='tok-ident'>{escaped code}</span> span, so the code
  is still rendered (without syntax highlighting). Update the
  existing unknown-language test to assert the fallback output
  and add two more tests (HTML-escape, empty-input edge case).

* fix: remove SyntaxHighlighting wrapper, use CodeBlock directly in home.py

Regression fix for the previous-round SyntaxHighlighting dedup: the
delegation approach (SyntaxHighlighting returning CodeBlock(...))
broke at runtime with the error 'Root Node of Component must be
instance of "Element"', because CodeBlock(...) returns a Component
instance, not an Element. The @define_component contract requires
the setup function to return an Element, since __init_component
reads _tag_name / _attrs / _children / etc. from the return value
to build the Component wrapper.

SyntaxHighlighting's only meaningful difference from CodeBlock was
a single pre-processing call (strip_multiline_text().strip() to
remove the leading indentation introduced by Python triple-quoted
string literals). The whole element-construction logic, the
SignalBase branching, the default 'text' lang, the highlight()
call, and the raw_html wrapping were all clones of CodeBlock.

Take option A from the review discussion: delete the
SyntaxHighlighting component file and have home.py call CodeBlock
directly with the _code() helper that performs the one-line
pre-processing. This:

- Eliminates the Element-vs-Component return-type mismatch
- Removes 100% of the duplicate rendering logic
- Drops the test file that asserted delegation (no longer
  applicable since the wrapper is gone)
- Renders all 6 code blocks on the home page identically to
  before (verified by static site generation: 6 <pre
  class='code-block'> elements, 4 language-bash / 1
  language-python / 1 language-toml)

Verified: 1245 unit tests pass, 0 pyright errors, ruff clean,
webcompy generate produces a working static site with the same
6 highlighted code blocks as before.

* fix: address PR #178 seventh-round review (spec mismatch, dead token file)

Two issues from the seventh-round OpenCode review of PR #178:

1. Spec/implementation mismatch in code-block spec (Must Fix)

   The 'highlight()' Requirement scenario 'Unknown language raises an
   error' stated that highlight() must raise LexerNotFoundError on an
   unknown language. The implementation in
   webcompy/ui/code_block/_highlight.py:13-19 instead catches the error
   and returns a single <span class="tok-ident"> with the code
   HTML-escaped. This fallback was deliberately introduced in
   commit 4311edb (fifth-round review) to prevent an unknown lang
   value from crashing the entire page render, and the test
   test_highlight_unknown_language_falls_back_to_raw was added to
   enforce the new behavior.

   Update the spec to describe the actual behavior: highlight() is
   the user-facing convenience that opts into a graceful fallback
   (covering both unknown languages and empty token lists), while
   get_lexer() still raises LexerNotFoundError for callers that need
   the error programmatically. The fallback rationale (preventing
   page crashes) is preserved as an explanatory note below the
   Scenario block.

2. Triple-maintenance token duplication in docs_app (Should Improve)

   docs_app/static/styles/tokens.css duplicated the light-mode token
   values from webcompy/ui/_styles/tokens.css as an offline/CDN
   fallback. The :root[data-theme="dark"] block was dead code: the
   framework's ThemeManager applies dark mode via a reactive
   <style data-webcompy-dynamic> element, not via a data-theme
   attribute, so the selector never matched at runtime. The file
   created a third token source (alongside tokens.css and
   webcompy/ui/theme/_tokens.py) that had to be kept in sync
   whenever a new token was added.

   Delete the duplicate file and the <link rel='stylesheet'
   href='/styles/tokens.css'> from docs_app/app.py. The framework
   already auto-injects /_webcompy-ui/index.css (which @imports
   /_webcompy-ui/tokens.css) as the single source of truth, so the
   docs site continues to have layered token defaults and reactive
   theme overrides without the duplication. Verified by static site
   generation: /_webcompy-ui/tokens.css contains the --color-bg
   definitions, and no /styles/tokens.css link remains in the
   rendered HTML.

Co-Authored-By: opencode <noreply@opencode.ai>

* fix(ci): read latest review-result marker instead of any marker in digest

The OpenCode review digest comment accumulates '<!-- REVIEW_RESULT: ... -->'
markers from every prior run in a folded <details> block plus a Review
History list. The 'Submit PR review based on result' step used

  if echo "$COMMENT_BODY" | grep -q '<!-- REVIEW_RESULT: changes_requested -->'; then

to decide the PR review state. The grep matched the substring anywhere in
the body, so as soon as the history contained even one 'changes_requested'
marker (e.g. from a previous round), the current 'approved' verdict was
overridden and the PR was filed as 'request-changes'. This is why PR #178
stayed at CHANGES_REQUESTED through its final approved review: the
digest body inherited five 'changes_requested' markers from earlier
rounds.

Fix by extracting the LAST marker via 'grep -oP | tail -1' (the same
pattern already used for the '<!-- REVIEWED_AT: ... -->' SHA) and
comparing the value explicitly. The current review's marker is always
appended at the end of the body (see the printf at line 797), so the
last match is always the current verdict.

Apply the same fix to the OLD_RESULT extraction on the OLD comment body
(line 761): the OLD body may itself be a folded digest, in which case
'head -1' picks up the OLDEST historical marker instead of the most
recent OLD verdict. The history badge then displays the wrong color.
Using 'tail -1' here makes the history badge reflect the OLD review's
actual verdict.

Verified the fix locally with a synthetic digest body containing
nine markers ending in 'approved': the new logic returns
'NEW_RESULT=approved' and the script would correctly call
'gh pr review --approve'.

🤖 Generated with opencode

Co-Authored-By: opencode <noreply@opencode.ai>

* fix: address PR #178 eighth-round review (Bash $ preservation, cleanup clarity)

Three issues from the eighth-round OpenCode review of PR #178:

1. Bash lexer dropped the $ prefix from variable tokens (Must Fix)

   In webcompy/ui/code_block/lexers/_bash.py the 'variable' handler
   did 'name = value[2:-1] if value.startswith("${") else value[1:]'
   which stripped the '$' or '${...}' wrapper. The Token was then
   yielded with just the bare name ('HOME', 'PATH'), so any Bash code
   block using variables rendered as 'echo HOME' instead of the
   faithful 'echo $HOME'. This affected every Bash code block in
   docs_app that used variables.

   Fix: emit the matched value as-is (the variable pattern already
   includes the '$' or '${...}' in the match), so the token value
   preserves the original syntax. The highlight() function then
   renders the value inside a <span class="tok-ident"> and the
   output matches the input.

   Update the spec scenario in openspec/specs/code-block/spec.md
   accordingly: 'Token(TokenType.IDENTIFIER, "$VAR")' instead of
   'Token(TokenType.IDENTIFIER, "VAR")', and add a note that the
   full '${NAME}' form is also preserved for braced variables.

   Update the two existing tests in tests/test_code_block_lexers.py
   to assert the '$HOME' and '${PATH}' token values. The
   previously-recorded spec text in the test docstring 'This is a
   regression test for PR #178 fifth-round review' stays accurate;
   the fix here is a separate correctness improvement.

2. _cleanup_consumers mixed browser/server concerns (Should Improve)

   webcompy/elements/_head.py:_cleanup_consumers used an
   'if ENVIRONMENT == "pyscript":' guard around
   '_remove_emitted_style_elements()' but unconditionally
   consumer_destroy'd _callback_consumers and _style_callbacks.
   The two dicts are only populated by append_style() and
   set_html_attr(), both of which already gate callback creation
   behind the same pyscript check. So on the server path the
   loops iterated empty dicts and the consumer_destroy import
   was loaded for no purpose.

   Restructure the function to early-return when not in the
   browser environment, then perform all cleanup steps in a
   single browser-only block. The behavior is unchanged
   (the server path was already a no-op in practice) but the
   intent — 'this whole method is browser-only cleanup' —
   is now explicit and the redundant import on the server
   is gone.

3. CSS duplicates and other items in the review (Notes, not
   addressed)

   The review also flagged .page-container and .demo-display-*
   classes as defined in both docs_app/static/styles/components.css
   (global/unlayered) and the corresponding Home/DemoDisplay
   scoped_style blocks. These are pre-existing patterns and the
   global definitions win the cascade, making the scoped_style
   versions effectively dead code. The reviewer classified
   these as 'Consider removing' (not blocking), so they are
   deferred for a follow-up cleanup PR.

Verified: 1245 unit tests pass, 0 pyright errors, ruff clean,
openspec validate code-block passes, webcompy generate succeeds.

  BashLexer().tokenize('echo $HOME')
  -> [BUILTIN 'echo', IDENTIFIER ' ', IDENTIFIER '$HOME']

  BashLexer().tokenize('echo ${PATH}')
  -> [BUILTIN 'echo', IDENTIFIER ' ', IDENTIFIER '${PATH}']

  highlight('echo $HOME', 'bash')
  -> '<span class="tok-builtin nb">echo</span>'

* feat: implement FEAT-005 Coupon Management

Add coupon CRUD operations to the Registrations module enabling
organizers to create, list, view, and revoke single-use invitation
coupon codes for ticketed events.

Domain:
- Coupon aggregate root with Create/Revoke lifecycle
- CouponId, CouponStatus, CouponCode, TicketTypeInfo value objects
- TicketTypeId in Shared.Kernel for cross-module identity
- CouponCreatedDomainEvent for outbox-based invitation emails

Use Cases (4 vertical slices):
- CreateCoupon: validates ticket types, expiry, generates unique code
- ListCoupons: returns coupon summaries with computed status
- GetCouponDetails: returns full coupon details including code
- RevokeCoupon: idempotent revocation, rejects redeemed coupons

Infrastructure:
- CouponEntityConfiguration with PrimitiveCollection for ticket type IDs
- RegistrationsDbContext, DI, and module endpoint wiring
- RegistrationsMessagePolicy maps domain event to module event
- IsCancelled added to TicketTypeDto (forward-compatible)
- IsEventActiveAsync added to IOrganizationFacade (stub for FEAT-003)

Tests:
- 13 domain tests covering all 12 acceptance scenarios (SC-001 to SC-012)
- CouponBuilder with fluent API for test setup
- CreateCouponHandlerTests for handler-level SC-006

* Use Registrations facade to fetch live event details for emails

Replaces the registration-time snapshot of event Name/WebsiteUrl
with a synchronous facade lookup at email send time. This way later
emails (cancellation, reconfirm) reflect the current event
configuration even if the team has updated it since registration.

- New IRegistrationsFacade in Module.Registrations.Contracts with
  GetTicketedEventEmailContextAsync(eventId) returning
  TicketedEventEmailContextDto(Name, WebsiteUrl)
- Implementation delegates to a new GetTicketedEventEmailContextQuery
  that reads from RegistrationsDbContext.TicketedEvents
- Registered as scoped in Registrations DI (mirrors the
  IOrganizationFacade pattern; no caching for now)

* Consolidate modules into Core project

* Fix messaging dispatch after module consolidation into Admitto.Core

Three root causes fixed:

1. QueueMessageDispatcher used mediator.SendAsync<ICommand> causing
   type-erasure — handler lookup failed at runtime. Added SendCommandAsync
   to IMediator with MakeGenericType resolution, mirroring the existing
   PublishDomainEventAsync pattern.

2. All three module DI setups scanned the same Admitto.Core assembly
   (Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly() returns the same dll for all modules
   after consolidation). Scrutor's default Append strategy registered each
   handler 3× → 3 activity log entries, 3 emails. Fixed with a custom
   TryAddEnumerableStrategy in all four Add*HandlersFromAssembly methods.

3. AddIntegrationEventHandlersFromAssembly was still scanning ALL handlers
   in Admitto.Core and registering them under each module's own key. The
   IntegrationEventRouter iterates every subscriber module, so Email handlers
   ran once per module (3×). Fixed by adding an optional namespaceAnchor
   parameter to all scan methods and passing typeof(DependencyInjection) from
   each module's DI setup — scoping each scan to its own namespace subtree.

* fix: guard TeamMembershipAuthorizationHandler against unauthenticated requests

Without the guard, an unauthenticated request to a RequireTeamMembership-protected
endpoint causes TeamMembershipAuthorizationHandler to call userContextAccessor.Current
before UserContextResolutionMiddleware has populated the context, throwing an
InvalidOperationException instead of returning 401.

Co-authored-by: Copilot <223556219+Copilot@users.noreply.github.com>

* fix: add admitto-api audience mapper to admitto-ui Keycloak client

Tokens issued for the admitto-ui client were missing aud: ["admitto-api"],
causing the API's JWT audience validation to reject them with 401. Added
an oidc-audience-mapper protocol mapper (matching the pattern used by
admitto-test-runner) to include admitto-api in access tokens.

Co-authored-by: Copilot <223556219+Copilot@users.noreply.github.com>

* fix: restore global security requirement in OpenAPI spec and regenerate Admin UI SDK

BearerSecuritySchemeTransformer was missing document.Security and
document.SetReferenceHostDocument() calls, so the generated spec had no
security requirements on operations. HeyAPI therefore omitted the security
array from generated SDK functions, causing getAccessToken to never be
called and all API requests to be sent without an Authorization header.

* workflow daemon-recovery-suite: archive-loader-graceful → done (PR #310 merged b8fd79ae)

* workflow daemon-recovery-suite: apply-status-fidelity → review (#311, recovered from agent stall)

* feat: apply ahab-daemon-lifecycle-commands (#309)

* ahab-daemon-lifecycle-commands: task 1 — declare Stop(ctx, opts) on Manager interface

Adds StopOptions{Timeout, Force} adjacent to Status and extends the
daemonize.Manager interface with Stop(ctx context.Context, opts StopOptions) error.
Adds the matching Windows stub. Mirrors StopOptions in the systemd package
to avoid an import cycle (daemonize imports systemd, not the reverse).
Adds Stop test seam to fakeDaemonManager.

Temp stubs on darwinManager.Stop, linuxManager.Stop, and systemd.Manager.Stop
satisfy the interface; real implementations land in tasks 2 (darwin) and 3
(linux). go vet ./... is green; existing tests still pass.

* ahab-daemon-lifecycle-commands: task 2 — darwin Stop with wait-for-exit

Adds launchctl.Manager.KillSignal helper (wrapping launchctl kill TERM/KILL),
darwinManager.Stop method, plus the testable stopWithKiller core driven via
the darwinKiller + clock interface seams.

Decision 4 idempotence: 'Could not find process' in launchctl output → nil.
Decision 6 wait-for-exit: poll Status every 250ms until Running == false or
opts.Timeout (defaulting to 10s; negative Timeout skips polling). On timeout
with Force=false, return wrapped error including PID. With Force=true, send
SIGKILL and re-poll up to 2s.

Note: spec task 2.1 named the helper 'unexported' but task 2.2 calls it from a
sibling package; resolved by exporting as KillSignal (matches existing
exported Kickstart helper). Tests in manager_darwin_stop_test.go cover all
six branches (a)-(f) named in task 2.3 — fake launchctl runner + fake clock,
deterministic.

* ahab-daemon-lifecycle-commands: task 3 — linux Stop with linger-gated Kickstart

Adds systemd.Manager.Stop using stopWith(clock seam), driving systemctl
--user stop --no-block then polling Status every 250ms until the unit
becomes inactive. Force=true escalates to systemctl kill --signal=SIGKILL
and re-polls up to 2s. linuxManager.Stop converts daemonize.StopOptions to
systemd.StopOptions before delegating.

Decision 3 linger gate: systemd.Manager.Kickstart now re-checks the linger
probe before invoking systemctl restart, surfacing the same actionable
loginctl enable-linger error as Install would.

Tests: TestSystemdStop covers (a)–(d), TestSystemdKickstart_LingerGate
covers both branches, TestLinuxStop (build-tag linux) covers the
delegation surface.

* ahab-daemon-lifecycle-commands: task 4b — PID-file collision check helpers

Adds ahab/daemon/daemonize/pidfile.go with readPIDFile, processAlive, and the
exported PIDCollisionCheck per Decision 7: a non-launchd live PID at
/tmp/ahab.pid distinct from launchd's reported PID is the foreground/zombie
collision the gateway-bind contract has to fail-fast on.

Decision 7 sub-rule: only fail when launchdPID > 0 AND pidFromFile !=
launchdPID AND processAlive(pidFromFile). Stale PID files and missing
files are not collisions.

The helpers readPIDFile and processAlive stay unexported (package-private —
callers go through PIDCollisionCheck). PIDCollisionCheck is exported for
the gateway/cli DaemonStartCmd handler to call.

* ahab-daemon-lifecycle-commands: task 4c — Install rewrites plist/unit on content diff

Decision 8: when an existing plist (darwin) or unit file (linux) differs
from the freshly rendered version, the prior bootstrap-only path errored
with 'Service already loaded'. The fix:

  - darwin: bootout the loaded service before bootstrapping the new content
    (no-op when service was not loaded). New installPlist helper + darwinInstaller
    test seam drive the four cases (a)-(d) deterministically.
  - linux: existing rewrite path now branches on Status.Running for the
    active/inactive split — restart for active, enable --now for inactive
    (per spec 4c.3). Previously it always ran restart, which silently
    failed when the unit was stopped before the rewrite.

The 'identical content → already installed' no-op path is unchanged.

Tests: TestDarwinInstall_RewritesOnDiff covers (a)-(d) on darwin with the
fake darwinInstaller; TestManager_Install (existing) extended to cover
both active and inactive rewrite branches on linux.

* ahab-daemon-lifecycle-commands: tasks 4 + 5 — DaemonStart/Stop/Restart CLI handlers

Adds three new Kong subcommands under DaemonCmd:

  - ahab daemon start   → Status check + PIDCollisionCheck + Kickstart
  - ahab daemon stop    → Status check + Stop(opts.Timeout, opts.Force)
  - ahab daemon restart → Status check + Stop + Kickstart

Each handler routes through *LaunchctlContext exclusively — never the
gateway or the cluster — so they work when the daemon is dead, which is
the recovery contract from ahab-cli-startup-modes.

--help text on the existing ahab start/stop is updated to disambiguate
the two layers per Decision 1: 'ahab stop' is the gateway-socket path,
'ahab daemon stop' is the service-manager path. The --daemon flag is
marked deprecated.

Test seam pidFilePath lets the lifecycle tests avoid reading the real
/tmp/ahab.pid (which on a developer box can contain a live unrelated
PID and trip the collision check). Tests also assert the no-gateway-dial
contract by ensuring Install/Uninstall are never invoked.

* ahab-daemon-lifecycle-commands: task 6 — register lifecycle commands as LaunchctlOnly

ModeTable extends with three new entries:

  daemon start   → LaunchctlOnly
  daemon stop    → LaunchctlOnly
  daemon restart → LaunchctlOnly

Per Decision 5, the three new handlers share the same launchctl-only
carve-out as install/uninstall/status/logs. This is the recovery
contract: the handlers shell out to launchctl/systemctl and MUST NOT
touch the gateway or cluster, so they work precisely when everything
else is broken.

Tests: TestModeFor extended; TestLifecycleCommands_NotMisclassified
adds a negative assertion that none of the three new commands silently
fall through to DaemonOnly, OperatorHTTP, ManifestFS, or VersionOnly.

* ahab-daemon-lifecycle-commands: tasks 7 + 8 — verification gate + openspec validation

7.1: make fmt && make test && go vet ./... — all green at branch HEAD.
7.2-7.4: manual smokes deferred to archive PR (require operator host with
the daemon actually installed; table-driven unit tests with darwinKiller /
fakeSystemctl / fakeDaemonManager seams cover all branches deterministically).

8.1-8.2: openspec validate --changes returns zero errors for the change;
openspec status confirms all 4 artifacts done.

* ahab-daemon-lifecycle-commands: record pre-archive review marker

Apply step 5 self-review pass — spec compliance and code quality both
clean. Records the branch HEAD SHA, the explicit spec deviations
(KillSignal exported, PIDCollisionCheck on daemonize package), and the
finalized Stop(ctx, opts StopOptions) signature so the four Tier 2
dependents in the daemon-recovery-suite workflow can bind to it.

* ahab-daemon-lifecycle-commands: add KillSignal to fakeLaunchctlInstaller (rebase fixup for loader-graceful merge)

* feat: apply ahab-daemon-status-fidelity (#311)

* ahab-daemon-status-fidelity: section 1 — launchctl status parsing rewrite

Replace the quoted-pid regex with structured state/pid/last-exit-code
parsers. Detect not-installed via launchctl list exit 113. Add a
cmdRunner test seam so TestStatus_Parsing covers all six structural
cases (running, stopped-clean, waiting, throttled, not-installed,
launchctl-list error) without shelling out.

* ahab-daemon-status-fidelity: section 2 — daemonize.Status carries State + LastExitCode

Extend Status with State and LastExitCode fields and route the macOS
mapping through a pure mapDarwinStatus helper. Source derivation now
distinguishes launchd / launchd (retry-pending) / launchd (stopped)
per Decision 1 of the spec. Added TestDarwinStatus covering all five
state combinations.

* ahab-daemon-status-fidelity: section 3 — systemd LastExitCode parity

Parse ExecMainStatus from systemctl show and surface it on
systemd.Status.LastExitCode; propagate through linuxManager into
daemonize.Status.LastExitCode. Source mapping unchanged (no
retry-pending analog on systemd). Added last_exit_code_populated test
case.

* ahab-daemon-status-fidelity: section 4 — KeepAlivePolicy enum

Replace PlistConfig.KeepAlive bool with KeepAlivePolicy enum (Never /
Always / OnCrash). The default install switches from Always (legacy
boolean true) to OnCrash (SuccessfulExit=false dict form), letting
launchd's standard throttle engage on misconfigured boots. Existing
golden plists now omit the KeepAlive key when policy is Never (zero
value); added keepalive_oncrash.plist golden.

* ahab-daemon-status-fidelity: section 5 — DaemonStatusCmd renders LastExitCode

Switch DaemonStatusCmd.Run to a four-arm switch that surfaces
LastExitCode on stopped/installed daemons ("installed (Source, last
exit N) but not running") while keeping the existing running and
not-installed lines unchanged. Three new test cases cover

* ahab-daemon-worktree-cleanup-on-stop: tasks 1+2 — JobQueue.CancelInFlight

Adds CancelInFlight(ctx, reason) (int, error) to the JobQueue interface
and implements it on sqliteQueue. The method SELECTs every non-terminal
job (pending/claimed/running/paused) and routes each through the
existing Transition(ctx, jobID, StateCancelled) so ValidTransition is
honored per row. Per-row failures are logged and skipped; the count
reflects only successful transitions.

Also introduces JobReasonDaemonShutdown = "daemon-shutdown" in
ahab/jobs/types.go, recorded on each cancelled row's error_message
column via a new unexported setErrorMessage helper.

Five test fakes that implement JobQueue receive a return-zero stub:
fakeJobQueue (cluster/jobrouting), mockQueue (runner), memQueue
(runner/integration), emptyQueue (daemon), nullJobQueue (gateway/http).

Tests in ahab/jobs/queue_cancel_inflight_test.go cover: mixed
in-flight + terminal queue, all-terminal, idempotent re-invocation,
empty queue, and verbatim reason recording.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

* ahab-daemon-worktree-cleanup-on-stop: tasks 3+4 — SIGTERM handler + Prom counter

Wires JobQueue.CancelInFlight into the daemon's SIGTERM/SIGINT handler
between signal-receipt and daemonCancel(). The call lives in a new
helper (Daemon.cancelInFlightOnShutdown) so tests can drive it
directly without launching real signals through the test runner.

Helper semantics:
- nil JobQueue is a clean no-op
- bounded by a fixed 5 s timeout (shutdownCancelInFlightTimeout) so a
  wedged DB cannot stall shutdown
- error logs at warn level, success logs at info with count
- on success, an optional Config.JobsCancelledOnShutdown counter is
  bumped by the returned count (nil-tolerant)

Adds the canonical Prometheus counter
PrometheusCollectors.JobsCancelledOnShutdownTotal (name
ahab_jobs_cancelled_on_shutdown_total) and wires it into the daemon
Config from ahab/cli/boot/daemon.go via promSub.Collectors().

The cluster-drain step from sister proposal ahab-daemon-cluster-drain
will land immediately above this call site; merge-time conflict
resolution falls to the orchestrator. A comment in daemon.go documents
the canonical drain → cancel-in-flight → daemonCancel ordering.

Tests in ahab/daemon/shutdown_cancel_test.go cover: happy path with
counter increment, error logged not propagated, zero-count path,
nil-queue no-op, nil-counter tolerated, and the 5 s deadline carried
on the inner context. Uses a recordingJobQueue stand-in that panics
on any non-CancelInFlight method to fail fast on accidental code-path
misuse.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

* ahab-daemon-plist-drift-warning: task 1 — drift detection in LaunchctlStatus

Add `PlistDrift` struct and `Manager.RenderFn` field to launchctl.
`Manager.computeDrift` SHA-256-hashes the on-disk plist and the
canonical render, populating `LaunchctlStatus.PlistDrift` from
`Manager.Status`. Nil when the file is absent, the renderer is
unwired, or the renderer errors — drift detection is informational
and never errors out a Status call.

Propagate the result onto `daemonize.Status.Drift` (platform-agnostic
boolean) via `mapDarwinStatus`. `NewManager` wires the production
`darwinManager.renderPlist` closure onto `launchctl.Manager.RenderFn`
so Status compares against the same bytes Install would write.

Tests cover the three structural cases (matches / differs / absent),
the render-error path, and the nil-RenderFn defensive path.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

* ahab-daemon-plist-drift-warning: task 2 — Linux systemd unit drift detection

Mirror the launchctl-side helper: `Manager.computeDrift` SHA-256-
compares the on-disk unit file with the canonical render, populating
`systemd.Status.Drift bool`. False on missing file / nil renderer /
render error — drift detection is informational on Linux too.

`linuxManager.Status` propagates the field onto `daemonize.Status.Drift`
so the cross-platform CLI can read a single boolean regardless of
platform.

Tests cover the four structural cases (matching / differing /
absent / render error) plus a Status-level integration test.

hand-edit case and the post-upgrade case where the canonical render

* ahab-daemon-plist-drift-warning: task 5 — JSON payload + Prometheus gauge

Add the daemon-side surface for the drift signal across three layers:

   `daemon_plist_drift,omitempty`) — non-breaking JSON addition.

2. `telemetry.PrometheusCollectors.PlistDriftGauge` plus
   `PrometheusSubscriber.SetPlistDrift(bool)`. Plain `prometheus.Gauge`
   per task 5.2 — no labels, single fleet-monitoring signal. (A

   antipattern.)

   function-typed hook so handleStatus can populate the JSON field

* feat: apply ahab-daemon-cluster-drain (#320)

* ahab-daemon-cluster-drain: task 1 — Transport.Flush interface extension

Add Flush(ctx) method to the cluster.Transport interface so callers
can confirm server-level receipt of preceding Publish calls.

- NATSTransport.Flush forwards to nats.Conn.FlushWithContext.
- MockTransport / fakePublisherTransport stubs return nil immediately
  (synchronous publish has no buffer to flush).
- New table-driven tests cover responsive-server flush success and
  already-cancelled-context error.

Per openspec/changes/ahab-daemon-cluster-drain Decision 2.

* ahab-daemon-cluster-drain: task 2 — Membership.Drain implementation

Add a Drain(ctx) method to the Membership interface and its NATS
implementation. Drain publishes the existing "leave" cluster event
(via publishEvent on the existing ahab.cluster.events subject — no
new subject, no payload shape change) and then calls Transport.Flush
to confirm server-level receipt. Bounded by
MembershipConfig.DrainTimeout; DrainTimeout=0 returns nil immediately
so operators can opt out of synchronous drain.

Five-case table-driven test covers: success path, wrapped publish
error, wrapped flush error, drain_timeout bound when flush blocks,
and DrainTimeout=0 short-circuit.

Per openspec/changes/ahab-daemon-cluster-drain Decision 1.

* ahab-daemon-cluster-drain: task 3 — drop post-cancel Leave from Serve

Remove the now-redundant fire-and-forget
  _ = n.membership.Leave(context.Background())
at the end of ClusterNode.Serve. After this PR the synchronous
Drain (called by the daemon's SIGTERM handler before context
cancellation) is the graceful-leave path. Crash paths (SIGKILL,
SIGSEGV) cannot drain anyway and continue to rely on heartbeat-
timeout discovery.

New test TestClusterNode_ServeDoesNotPublishPostCancelLeave pins
the invariant: cancelling Serve's context without Drain produces
no leave event on ahab.cluster.events.

Per openspec/changes/ahab-daemon-cluster-drain Decision 3.

* ahab-daemon-cluster-drain: task 4 — daemon graceful-shutdown drain hook

Wire the cluster drain step into the daemon's graceful-shutdown path:

- Add Config.Drainer (interface) and Config.DrainTimeout fields. The
  cluster boot wiring populates them with cluster.ClusterNode.Drain
  and the parsed [cluster] drain_timeout (next task).
- Refactor SIGTERM handler and programmatic Stop to share a
  drainCluster() helper that runs the drain BEFORE cancelling the
  daemon's top-level context. Drain failure is logged at warn level
  and does not block cancellation.
- Forward Drain(ctx) on cluster.ClusterNode to membership.Drain so the
  boot wiring has a clean concrete to bind to Config.Drainer.

Four-case table-driven test covers: drain runs pre-cancel; drain
failure does not block; DrainTimeout=0 skips; nil Drainer skips.

Per openspec/changes/ahab-daemon-cluster-drain Decision 4.

* ahab-daemon-cluster-drain: task 5 — config + boot wiring

Plumb [cluster] drain_timeout from TOML through to MembershipConfig
and the daemon Drainer hook:

- ClusterConfig.DrainTimeout is *TOMLDuration so an absent field can
  be distinguished from explicit "0s" (operator opt-out). Mirrors
  the *bool Enabled pattern.
- ApplyClusterDefaults fills 5s when DrainTimeout is nil; explicit
  "0s" stays 0 (legacy heartbeat-timeout discovery).
- cluster.ClusterNodeConfig.DrainTimeout passes the value into
  MembershipConfig.DrainTimeout so Membership.Drain is bounded by
  the operator-configured budget.
- daemon.Daemon.SetDrainer registers the cluster node as the daemon
  Drainer; cli/boot/daemon.go calls SetDrainer right after
  NewClusterNode so the SIGTERM/Stop path drains before context
  cancellation.

Three-case TOML round-trip test covers explicit 10s, explicit 0s
opt-out, and absent-field-defaults-to-5s.

Per openspec/changes/ahab-daemon-cluster-drain task 5.

* ahab-gateway-bind-failfast: task 1 — bind-before-log refactor

Refactor Server.serve to call net.Listen synchronously before logging
'listening on', so the log line is honest. On bind failure, return
wrapBindError immediately — the goroutine is never started, suture
sees a permanent error, and the wrapper (task 3) can fail-fast.

Replaced ListenAndServe() with Serve(listener) on the goroutine path
and use listener.Addr().String() so :0 test addresses log the
actually-bound port.

Add stub bind_error.go (full impl in task 2) and TestServer_Serve_LogOrdering
locking in the bind-before-log invariant from spec scenario 1.

* ahab-gateway-bind-failfast: task 2 — wrapBindError + port-owner probe

Add bind_error_test.go covering the three message-format branches
(non-EADDRINUSE wraps with %w, EADDRINUSE with PID names PID/recovery,
EADDRINUSE without PID still names the addr) plus a probePortOwner
smoke test that opens a real listener and verifies the probe returns
os.Getpid() — skipping cleanly when the lsof/ss binary is absent.

Adds 2-second context.WithTimeout on the probe so a hung lsof/ss
cannot block the error return, and graceful-degrade returns 0 on
parse / timeout / missing-binary failures.

* ahab-gateway-bind-failfast: task 3 — suture wrapper for EADDRINUSE

Add wrapGatewayService that triggers app.Daemon.Stop on bind-class
errors (errors.Is EADDRINUSE, with string-fallback for wrapper
flattening) and wire it into StartCmd.Run at the AddService call
site. The wrapper preserves the inner error byte-for-byte so suture
still logs the root cause; the only side effect is the onFatal call
that cancels the daemon's top-level context, ending the supervision
tree before suture's restart policy can fire.

Non-bind errors flow through unchanged so transient gateway
failures (e.g. downstream Ollama blip) keep their existing
restart-friendly behavior.

* ahab-gateway-bind-failfast: task 4 — daemon-level bindfail integration test

TestBindConflict_DaemonExits builds a real *daemon.Daemon, AddService's
a wrapped fake gateway-shaped Service that returns syscall.EADDRINUSE,
and asserts daemon.Run returns within 5s — proving the wrapper's
onFatal call cancels the daemon's top-level context instead of
letting suture's restart policy enter a tight retry loop.

Negative-control verification (identity wrapper, no Stop) confirmed
daemon.Run hangs past 2s without the wrapper; with the wrapper it
returns in 0.00s. The test is load-bearing.

Note: per task 4.1's implementation note, we construct the daemon
directly rather than the full AppContext (which requires plugin
registry / store DB / router) — same wrapper-→Stop-→Run-returns
plumbing exercised under suture supervision.

* ahab-gateway-bind-failfast: tasks 5-6 — verification gates and spec validation

End-to-end project gate (make fmt && make test && go vet ./...)
all green. Manual smoke (task 5.2) deferred: running a live
ahab daemon start from the apply worktree risks PID-file collision
with the user's main-checkout daemon, and the fail-fast path is
already covered by TestBindConflict_DaemonExits with a negative
control. Recommended for operator validation on a fresh pequot
node before tagging.

* fix(docker): upload volume 改 tmpfs(修 distroless nonroot 写权限问题导致 native smoke FAIL)

- 原 mount:`- seafood_uploads:/app/var/seafood/uploads`(named volume 首次挂载 root-owned mount point)

- 启动期 UploadWritabilityChecker(commit 3ca8a22 引入)check writability → IllegalStateException → native binary 拉崩 → smoke FAIL — actuator/health 30s 内返 000000

- 改 mount:tmpfs + size 100M。docker compose 默认 mode 1777(world-writable + sticky),nonroot UID 65532 可写,check 通过

- 数据非持久(容器重启丢失)— 对 CI smoke / 短期 dev OK

- 持久化路径:取消 volumes: 段 seafood_uploads 注释 + 加 busybox init 容器 chown -R 65532:65532 <mount point>,在 service depends_on 加 init 容器(compose 文件注释已说明)

- sessionStorage 持久化锁定状态,refresh 后倒计时继续(同会话内)

- 倒计时归零自动解锁;成功登录时主动清锁定

- 锁定中按钮文案: "锁定中 (MM:SS)", 输入框 + 复选框全禁用

* fix(sprint-1-closure): 修 LoginLockoutService read-after-write 竞态,4 次失败即可锁

原 recordFailure 返 void;controller 只能在 catch 块调一次,等下次
isIpLocked 检查时,read-after-write 竞态让 4th attempt 仍走 catch
DomainException → 409 而非 423/429。

修:recordFailure 改返 boolean=true 当且仅当本次失败推到了锁阈值;controller
看到 true 立即返 423/429,绕过下次 read-after-write 竞态路径。

同时:Jest lockout UX 修复(login-status 透传)与之配套,前端 4th attempt
就能看到 423 + 倒计时。

## 修 mp 端后端数据流 2 个 bug

A. utils/request.js 启动时 getApp()=undefined
   模块加载阶段调 getApp() 拿不到 app instance(WeChat mp 限制),
   导致 'Cannot read property globalData of undefined'。
   改为延迟到 request() 调用时拿,safe fallback。

B. src/api/product.js 不识别 Spring Data Page<T> 形状
   backend 返 { content, totalElements, totalPages, number, ... }
   v1 code 找 response.products → 0 数据
   加 converter(content → products + 算 hasNext/hasPrev)

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 <noreply@anthropic.com>

* fix(backend): admin dashboard 403 修复 — cookie auth 双层 bug

本次 ad-02 happy state 视觉验收时连续 403,深挖 2 个真 bug:

## Bug 1: JwtAuthenticationFilter 不读 cookie
原实现只解析 'Authorization: Bearer' header,完全忽略 httpOnly cookie。
admin-ui 走 cookie 登录,filter 拿不到 token → 403。
修:加 ADMIN_COOKIE 常量 + extractToken() 兜底读 seafood_admin_token cookie
(admin path only;user 端目前无 cookie auth)。

## Bug 2: Set-Cookie 没 Domain 属性
写 cookie 时不设 Domain,默认绑在响应 host (8080)。
dev 下 admin-ui (5173) 与 backend (8080) 端口不同,浏览器 5173 请求不带 8080 的 cookie。
修:加 @Value admin.cookie.domain(默认空,prod 留空按 host 绑;dev 配 localhost 跨端口共享)。
ResponseCookieBuilder 加 domain() 方法 + build() 串入。

## 修后验证
- POST /cookie-login 返 204 + Set-Cookie: seafood_admin_token=...; Domain=localhost
- GET /api/admin/dashboard with cookie 返 200 (之前 403)
- Playwright 浏览器: 4 KPI 真实值(在售商品 43,共 50 款 · 缺货 0) +
  7 天趋势 + 库存预警(海葡萄 2 / 大闸蟹 6 / 金枪鱼 8) + 分类分布(贝/鱼/虾/软/藻)全可见

admin-dashboard-actual.png 截图归档。

* fix(sprint1-closure): v2.1 6 屏截图期间发现 4 个真 bug,修 2 个

1. 静态资源 403 — SecurityConfig/StaticResourceConfig
   根因:vite 默认 base='/' → 产物 index.html 引用 /assets/... 根路径
   但 Security 只 permitAll /admin/** → /assets/** 走 authenticated() 返 403
   修:加 /assets/** permitAll + addResourceHandler(classpath:/static/admin/assets/)
   影响:admin SPA 完整加载(JS/CSS/字体)

2. TokenResponse mask 成 'eyJh***'
   根因:SensitiveValueBeanSerializerModifier 全局按字段名脱敏,accessToken/refreshToken
   命中 token 子串被 mask → 前端拿到的 token 是 'eyJh***' → 所有 admin API 必 403
   修:shouldMask 加字段名白名单(accessToken/refreshToken/role/username)
   验证:dashboard 返 200 真实 payload(50 商品/43 在售/7d trend/lowStock)

未修但已发现的 2 个 admin bug(留 Sprint 2):
3. admin-ui OrderListPage 调 /api/orders(mp 端)而不是 /api/admin/orders
4. AdminOrderController 缺 GET /{id}/detail 端点,OrderDetailPage 调了不存在的端点

admin 6 屏截图(ad-01~06)commit:
- ad-01 login: 0 errors
- ad-02 dashboard: 4 KPI + 7d trend + 近期订单 + 低库存
- ad-03 products: DataTable 50 商品/分类 tab/状态 tab
- ad-04 product form: 新建商品 dialog 9 字段
- ad-05 orders: error state(OrderListPage 端点 bug)
- ad-06 order detail: error state(backend 缺 endpoint)

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 <noreply@anthropic.com>

* fix(mp): v2.1 验证发现 mp 6 屏布局/数据 bug,本轮修 4 个

根因分析(debug 抓的现场数据):
- products.length=0, isEmpty=true, errorMessage='未知错误'
- categories 4 个正常,banners 字段 undefined
- consoleLogs 0,consoleErrs 0 — 静默失败

修 1:utils/request.js — GET 请求过滤 undefined / null / '' 字段
  wx.request GET 模式把 data 拼到 URL query,未过滤的 undefined
  变成 ?category=undefined 字面量,backend 找不到匹配返空。这是 home
  page 6 卡瀑布空白 / order-list 0 单的根因。

修 2:src/modules/productList/productList.js getErrorMessage 默认值
  原:'未知错误' — 在 isError=false 时也返,UI 误显。
  现:'' 空字符串,无 error 不显示。

修 3:6 个页面 json usingComponents 注册 shared-empty / shared-loading
  原:usingComponents: {} 或缺失,wxml 里的 <shared-empty><shared-loading>
  全部不渲染。空 loading/empty/error 状态看不到。
  涉及:index/category/cart/profile/order-list/product-detail
  注册指向 @vant/weapp/empty/index + @vant/weapp/loading/index(Skyline 兼容)。

修 4:pages/index/index.wxss .home-chips 加 overflow-x: auto
  原:white-space: nowrap 单独不够,scroll-view 在 Skyline 下默认
  overflow:hidden,inline-flex chip 会按 block 排版重叠。
  现:display: block + overflow-x: auto + overflow-y: hidden,横滚生效。

已 commit 的 mp-01-home-v2.1 截图显示:products 正常渲染(6 卡瀑布
20 个商品加载成功),6 卡 2 列 grid + 价格 + 加号按钮可见。
剩余未修:banners 字段缺失 → swiper 不显示(Sprint 2);其他 6 屏截图
受 DevTools WebSocket 间歇 stall 限制未重截(stability.md 已给模板)。

新增 4 个 _debug-*.cjs 脚本(dev only,gitignored future)+ _visual-after-fix.cjs
工作脚本,可在 miniprogram-automator WebSocket stall 间歇环境下逐屏截图。

* test(order): OrderControllerSliceTest — 4 cases (CUSTOMER list/get, ADMIN ship, 403 ship-as-CUSTOMER)

- list_asCustomer_returnsPagedOrders: stub PageImpl + JSON content[0].id 校验
- get_notFound_returns404: stub list() 返空页(控制器 get 实际走 list + filter),断言 { code } 404 body
- ship_asCustomer_returns403: SecurityContextHolder 注入 CUSTOMER + @PreAuthorize hasRole(ADMIN) → 403
- ship_asAdmin_returnsShippedOrder: ADMIN 覆盖 context,stub service.ship(),200 + body.id

@WebMvcTest 切片下 Spring Security 7 两个坑:
1. SecurityContextHolderFilter 不会从 SecurityMockMvcRequestPostProcessors 保存的
   请求属性读回 SecurityContext → @PreAuthorize 拿不到 Authentication
   抛 AuthenticationCredentialsNotFoundException。解法:@BeforeEach 直接写
   SecurityContextHolder thread-local。
2. AccessDeniedExceptionResolver(SS7 extendHandlerExceptionResolvers 注册)在
   @WebMvcTest 切片里 register 时机不对,AuthorizationDeniedException 冒泡成
   ServletException。解法:@TestConfiguration 内嵌 @RestControllerAdvice
   @ExceptionHandler(AccessDeniedException.class) → 403 兜底。

Co-Authored-By: Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>

* test(shared): fix SensitiveValueBeanSerializerModifierTest.masksFieldNamedToken flake

Root cause: test fixture used 'accessToken' field name, but
SensitiveValueBeanSerializerModifier#shouldMask has explicit whitelist
(accessToken/refreshToken/role/username) — these are API DTO fields that
must return tokens un-masked (otherwise admin API gets 403).
By-design unmasked, not a bug.

Change fixture field to 'privateToken' — still matches 'token' regex,
not in whitelist, exercises real mask path. Input to 200+ char real JWT
length, validates mask truncation logic.

10/10 rerun PASS, deterministic.

🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)

* build(pitest): C1 变异测试接入 + nightly job + 70% gate(基线 72%)

承接 Sprint 4 C1(原因兼容性未验证被推迟)。spike 证明 PIT 1.22.1 在
JDK 25(class file v69)+ Gradle 9 + Jupiter 6 完整跑通——C1 推迟所担心的
ASM/字节码兼容性并非真障碍。

真实根因(3 层,均与 JDK25/ASM 无关):
1. `-x processTestAot` 触发 pitest classpath provider 提前查询 → 禁用
   processTestAot(本项目常规 test 本就一律 -x 跳过,且其自身因下述错位无法运行)
2. coverage minion(裸 forked JVM)吃 testRuntimeClasspath 上的
   junit-platform-launcher 1.12.2,与 engine 6.0.3 错位 → OutputDirectoryCreator 缺失
3. 该错位由 Spring Boot 4.0.6 依赖管理 rule 钉死,force/dependencyManagement
   均被反盖 → 用 dependencySubstitution 无条件替换 launcher 至 6.0.3
   (副带修复:processTestAot 与常规 test 的同源错位)

配置(design D3/D4):
- 作用域白名单 order/product 的 domain+application,排除 dto/Document/main
- 实测核心包基线变异分 72%(357 变异/杀 257,Line 82%,Test strength 87%)→ gate 设 70
- gate 强制力已验证:threshold=95 → "score 72 below threshold" + 非零退出
- 不进 check 主链;nightly.yml 新增 pitest job,报告 artifact 留 30 天
- README 加 mutation badge + 说明

回归:`./gradlew test` 仍 BUILD SUCCESSFUL(launcher 改 6.0.3 未破坏测试)

* build(pitest): PR-scoped 增量变异 gate(对齐 roadmap PR-gating)

承接用户选项:在 nightly 全量 70% gate 之外,补 PR 内对改动核心模块的增量
变异 gate(roadmap test-roadmap spec "gates PR merge")。

实施中发现每模块基线严重不均,统一 70% 不可行:
  order.domain 90% / order.application 80% → order 合并 83%
  product.domain 73% / product.application 32% → product 合并仅 43%
product.application 50% 行覆盖、50/74 变异无覆盖 = 已知测试债。

按模块基线 floor 落地(用户定,grandfather 现状只防回退):
- build.gradle 参数化 -PpitScope=order|product|all(map 驱动 classes/tests/threshold)
  order floor 80 / product floor 40 / all 70。默认 all 与重构前字节一致(pitest FROM-CACHE 佐证)
- ci.yml 新增 pitest-incremental job(仅 PR):git diff 检测改动模块 → scoped 跑 → 按 floor 卡门;
  无核心改动则 exit 0 跳过。安全:run 块用内置 $GITHUB_BASE_REF,无 github.event.* 插值
- mutation-testing spec:拆全量(nightly)+ 新增 PR 增量 gate(per-module floor)requirement

实测:pitScope=order → 83% 过 80;pitScope=product → 43% 过 40;均 EXIT=0

遗留(子项目 A 续,tasks 6.5):product.application 补测至 ≥70% 后,product floor 40 → 70

* test(domain): C4 property-based — jqwik 覆盖 decrementStock/OrderStatus/构造校验

承接 sprint-5-c4-property-testing。

§1 库选型 spike(go/no-go,通过):jqwik 1.10.1 引擎绑 junit-platform 1.14.x,
项目在 6.0.3(差 5 个大版本),但 TestEngine SPI 未崩 —— spike 探针(0.6s 跑上千组
样本)证明 jqwik 在 junit-platform 6.0.3 上正常发现执行,不需 QuickTheories 回退。

§2-3 13 个 domain property(jqwik 随机样本逼边界反例):
- ProductDecrementStockProperties(3):∀ 0<qty≤stock → stock-qty 且 ≥0;超量/非正 → DomainException
- OrderStatusProperties(3):∀ 状态 of(code()) round-trip 恒等;终态不可流出;无自环
- ProductConstructionProperties(7):Sku/Product ∀ 合法构造成功;∀ name>100/price≤0/stock<0 → DomainException

纯 test-scope,零生产改动;全量 ./gradlew test 绿,jqwik 与 jupiter 引擎并存无扰动。
